Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The stereochemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 179 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 355 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 179 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stereochemistry structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 355. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
